CohortAction by mission/DN Client Enquiries for When clients send a client enquiry to a mission e-mail address directly though their file is processed by DN, the DN casemission should provide a generic response that directs the client to send their enquiry via the in-Canada Webform

### [https://secure.cic.gc.ca/enquiries-renseignements/canada-case-cas-eng.aspx](https://secure.cic.gc.ca/enquiries-renseignements/canada-case-cas-eng.aspx). From that link, the client enquiry

will go through the CSC triage and to DN. If a client sends their enquiry through the Webform and it ends up in GN but it is a DN case, mission should forward the message to DN (IRCC.NCSUInternal-UNSCInterne.IRCC@cic.gc.ca). If the e-mail is too large to transmit or contains Protected B information, please upload the enquiry into GCMS instead.

Client Enquiries for If a IP enquiry gets sent to DN, DN should forward the enquiry to ACCRAIM@international.gc.ca. IP can also query incoming docs as CSC will upload these into the file.

1. Inbox: where you process/fetch Webforms.
2. New Email: allows you to send texts to clients/representatives.
3. Favourites: where you will find a list of texts you have saved.
4. Search: allows you to search for an email that you or another agent sent to a particular client/representative.
5. Language: changes the language of eGEM from/to English or French.
6. New email: another function that allows you to send texts to clients/representatives.
7. Emails Created Today: allows you to view any emails that you have created/drafted that same day.
## 8. Emails Sent Today: allows you to view any emails sent that same day.

9. eGEM Status: Similar to VCC, ensure your eGEM status reflects your current action/LoB.

How to Transfer a Webform To transfer a Webform to another LoB:

Update the LoB and Sub-LoB fields to reflect the correct information Select Transfer. If you do not have the updated LoB registered to your eGEM profile, it will be removed from your inbox.

# If you do have the updated LoB registered to your eGEM profile, it will

remain in your inbox for processing.

To transfer a Webform to a partner, such as OSC for a change of address request update the LoB field to Transfer to Partner update the Sub-LoB field to OSC – Change of Address or any other relevant partner Select Transfer. It will be removed from your inbox.

# Privacy & ESU

#### Privacy vs Authorization

### Privacy: requires that the client be adequately identified by providing the required

identifiers before any personal information is released.

The required identifiers are: Full Name

## Date of birth

■1 piece of case-specific information

### Authorization: the action or act of giving someone legal permission to access the

file(s). The person authorized MusT stili meet privacy by providing the required identifiers for the client in question.

## Which parts of the Webform can be used to pass privacy?

## Authorization & ESU

## Who is Authorized to Obtain Information?

A) Those involved in the application: The principal applicant (PA): including, but not limited to, a Relay-Bell operator for hearing impaired clients. **Important Note: In a sponsorship application, the sponsor (SPR) has access to their own application (request to sponsor) but not that of the person they are sponsoring (the PA) – SPR must submit a signed IMM5475 or IMM5476. A dependant of the applicant (DEP): if listed on the same application (e.g., application for PR).
B) Paid Representatives **Must have submitted a signed IMM5475 or IMM5476** Lawyers, and students-at-law under their supervision; ■Notaries, and students-at-law under their supervision; or Immigration/citizenship consultants.
C) Unpaid Representatives **Must have submitted a signed IMM5475 or IMM5476** A family member or friend; Non-government organizations (NGOs); Religious organizations; Other third parties
C) Parents/legal guardian(s) ** No additional forms are required** Parents/legal guardian(s):
- For minor children; or
- Those that were minors at the time of application and have not requested to rescind their authorization.
D) Custodians of minor children **Must have submitted a signed IMM5646 ** Custodians of minor children: proof of custodianship (IMM5646) should be available in GCMS ESU Accommodation Training – Participant Guide v3Prepared by M. de Lima

## What is Personal Information?

As per section 3 of the Privacy Act, personal information means information that is recorded in any form that can identify an individual.

Some examples are: First, middle and last names

## Address (residential/mailing)

Phone number and email address Any identifying number or symbol (e.g., SIN, UCI, application #, etc.) Application file status

# Fingerprints

More examples can be found here.

### Privacy & Authorization-Minors

## Reporting a Privacy Breach

Path Procedures > Email Support Unit > Reporting > Privacy Breach

What is a Privacy Breach? A privacy breach is the loss of, unauthorized access to, or disclosure of, personal

# information. Breaches can happen when personal information is stolen, lost or

mistakenly shared.

Examples

# You provided information to an unauthorized party;

■Documents are uploaded to an application belonging to someone other than the owner of the information; ■A client received the correspondence of one or numerous other clients; A client accessed an online tool and the information displayed belongs to another individual; A client received a phone call form IRCC and were given information about one or numerous other clients.

The client informs us of a privacy breach

1. Process the Webform as usual; if applicable, take the required actions (e.g., send a referral, add a note, send an eGEM text);

3. Add a note in the system; and

## Robotic Process Automation (RPA)

### What is Robotic Process Automation (RPA)?

## Robotic Process Automation, herein referred to as RPA, is a code/script that assists

ESU agents by creating an Incoming Correspondence (IC) within the application and then attaching the document(s) provided in a Webform.

Currently, there are 4 bots attaching documents on a daily basis at the CSC and more will be added as inventory is further deployed on eGEM.

There are currently three (3) distinct strategies used at ESU involving RPA:

1. Pre-Agent Bot Action
2. Post-Agent Bot Action
# 3. Uncoupling

We will review each in depth further on in this document.

ESU Accommodation Training – Participant Guide v3Prepared by M. de Lima 000035

and Cizenship Canedaot Citoyenneté Canada Information disclosed under the Access to Information Act L'information divulguée en vertu de la loi sur l'accés à Tinformalio

What can be processed by RPA?

# Webforms with Low priority documents (those with an Incoming Correspondence

due date of 20 business days).

The Webform must also meet the following criteria:

1. Application number: must appear in the File Number field of the Webform. The following formats can be accepted:
- F000123123 - INLAND SCLP;
- XS00000987 (Prospective applications-RPA will verify both with and without the “X”).
2. Privacy must be met.
3. Unsolicited documents.
4. Solicited documents for which no line has been created in Correspondence-Incoming view.
5. Open/ongoing IR application
6. In-Canada applications For closed applications: the document should only be attached if it brings value
i.e., Medical exam completed after the application was closed.
# 7. Webforms received in Technical format as long as:

- The application number must be indicated in the correct field.
- The link leads to an unsolicited document.
ESU Accommodation Training – Participant Guide v3 Prepared by M. de Lima 000036

Immigration, Réfugiés and Cizenship Canedaot Cioyenneté Canada Information disclosed under the Access to Information Act L'information divulguée en vertu de la loi sur l’accès à Tinformatior

What cannot be processed by RPA?

# Webforms with HIGH priority documents (those with an Incoming Correspondence

due date of LESS THAN 20 business days).

1. Webforms without an application number in the File Number field.
a. i.e., application number appears in the Enquiry section but not in the File Number field.
2. Solicited documents for which a line has been created in Correspondence-Incoming by the officer in GCMS.
3. Closed IR files – advise the enquirer to contact the Visa Office.
4. Entering payment receipts – follow this procedure.
5. Webforms which require that you transfer to Use of Representative.
6. Requests that require action in less than 20 business days:
- Withdrawals
- Reconsiderations
- Requests for more time to provide a document
- Urgent requests
7. Updated information requests.
# 8. Bundles (aka Grouped emails) – you must isolate and unbundle the Webform

# in question (only applies to the Post-Agent Bot Action strategy).

Agents' role in the Pre-Agent Bot Action Strategy

The following verifications must be completed by the ESU agent receiving a Webform that is part of the Pre-Agent Bot Action Strategy.

1. Verify privacy-privacy is considered NoT met when the enquirer fails to provide the necessary identifiers or when RPA uploads the document(s) to an incorrect client's application. Example: The document belongs to Mr. Smith but it is uploaded to the application of Mrs. Jones (with no common group #).
A. If privacy is NOT MET
1. Access the application that the document was incorrectly uploaded to
2. Update the IC status to “cancelled”
3. Send the referral ESU-RPA Upload Mix-Up
4. Reply to the enquirer with the text Incorrect info-Little to no impact for client (RPA upfront upload strategy) along with any other applicable text(s)
5. Enter the appropriate wrap-up code
6. Click Process to finalize the Webform.
B. If privacy IS MET: continue to the next step
2. Was the document uploaded to the correct application? Example: The document is for the correct client but uploaded to their TR application when document is meant for their PR application.
A. Document(s) uploaded to the wrong application:
1. Access the application the document was incorrectly uploaded to
2. Update the IĆ status to “cancelled”
# 3. If you have enough information, upload the document to the correct

application and reply with the appropriate text(s).

4. If you do not have enough information, reply to the enquirer with the text Incorrect info-Little to no impact for client (RPA upfront upload strategy) along with any other applicable text(s)
## 5. Enter the appropriate wrap-up code

6. Click Process to finalize the Webform.
B. Document(s) uploaded to the correct application: continue to the next step.

# Agents' role in the Post-Agent Action strategy

1. Verify privacy
- If privacy is not met, do not continue and respond with the applicable Missing Identifiers text.
- If privacy is met, continue to the next step
## 2. Verify authorization

- If the enquirer is not authorized and they are:
i. Only providing document(s), you may continue as long as the document is eligible for RPA and ensure you reply with “Document attached – Unauthorized third Party” text. ii. Providing documents and requesting additional information, you may continue as long as the document is eligible for RPA but you can only respond to requests for general information as they are not authorized to obtain case-specific information.
For documents provided and general enquiries, respond with “Document attached – Unauthorized third Party” + any applicable text(s) to answer the enquirer's general question.

For documents provided and case-specific enquiries/requests, respond with “Document attached – Unauthorized third Party”.

- If the enquirer is authorized – continue to the next step.
3. Ensure the correct application number has been provided and is present in the File Number field.
A. If the wrong application number was provided OR the application number is not present in the File Number field and you have enough information, upload the document to the correct application and reply with the appropriate text(s).
B. If the correct application number is provided in the File Number field, continue to the next step.
4. Verify if the document is eligible for RPA: Consult the sections in this document entitled What can be processed by RPA and/or What cannot be processed by RPA:
A. If the document is eligible, continue to the next step.
B. If the document is not eligible, add the document manually and do not continue with RPA.

# Uncoupling Strategy

In November 2023, the Webform was modified so that when a client/enquirer chooses to upload documents the free text box will no longer appear. This means that if the client/enquirer wishes to upload documents and ask general or case-specific questions, they will have to submit 2 Webforms:

One to submit documents and then; A second to ask any additional questions (to do so, they will need to select No when answering the question Do you want to add a document to an existing file/application?).

## This strategy applies to clients/enquirers submitting Webforms related to the following

lines of business:

## Temporary Residence (applied online) Temporary Residence (applied by mail)

In-Canada Permanent Residence Permanent Residence (applied online) Permanent Residence (applied by mail) Sponsorship

## Permanent Resident Card

Citizenship Replacement documents, Amendments to documents and Verification of Status documents

Impact – This will minimize the amount of Webforms agents will receive with attachments, allowing them to focus on answering questions and leveraging RPA to attach most documents.

More information can be found in WebCART > Email Support Unit > Robotic Process Automation (RPA) – Uncoupling strategy

1. Toggle Inbox: a view of your “inbox” of all the Webforms currently in your Alpha folder.
2. Fetch multiple emails: allows you to fetch multiple Webforms at a time.
3. Load emails: allows you to fetch one Webform at a time.
4. EN: open an email draft in English
### 5. FR: open an email draft in French.

6. Code: a complete list of wrap up codes from which you can select the appropriate code for your email.
7. Hold: allows for an email to be placed on or removed form hold it may be processed (to be used when awaiting guidance or Quality approval).
8. Transfer: allows you to transfer or copy a message to another LOB or back to Question Box (Inventory).
9. Reject: allows you to reject an email (should only be done if advised by procedure). ESU Accommodation Training – Participant Guide v3Prepared by M. de Lima
000050

mmigration, Réfugiés et Citoyenneté Canada Information disclosed under the Access to Information Act L'information divulguée en vertu de la [oi sur l'accès à Finformatior

10. Process: to be used once all necessary actions are taken (as per procedure).

11. Quick reply: access scripts.

12. Options: access and modify Alpha parameters & also to submit an exception.
13. Script assistant: a list of suggested scripts that may be applicable.
14. Attachments: list of attachments included in the clients' Webform. You can copy the list of the name of the attachments by clicking on the word “Attachments”.

15. Auto Copy: allows you to copy information from the Webform (UCl, File Number, PA Name, Enquirer Name, etc.).

16. Audit Log: Chronological record of actions/activities since fetching the Webform.
17. HighRiskLoB: signals that the Webform is part of a Contentious LoB.

18. Priority Keyword: flagged with a priority keyword implemented due to an urgent/crisis situation (example: Iran2023).
19. Notes: when in green it means that it may contain important notes on how to proceed.

20. History: an audit trail of the Webform's history.

#### Alpha Best Practices

#### 1. Ensure settings are set to fetch no more than 10 Webforms at a time.

#### 2. When possible, fetch more than 1 email at a time.

### 3. When possible, do not have any other Excel-based programs open.

4. When possible, do not leave any Webforms in your Alpha at the end of your shift and advise your TL if ever Webforms should be removed.
## How to Process a Webform in Alpha

#### 1. Click the Fetch button to load emails

# 2. Determine if the Webform meets privacy

3. Determine the enquirer's needs and if they are authorized on file. 4. If applicable, take action on file.
#### 5. Respond to the enquirer

#### Grouped Webforms

Used as an inventory reduction strategy, Webforms sent by the same email address (Enquirer) are grouped together so that the enquirer in question receives one (1) response to all enquiries they have sent over a certain period.

The group of emails is also referred to as a “Bundle”.

### Bundled Items Type: Email and Identifier

Privacy and Authorization Privacy issues may arise due to the inconsistency of the clients' information. This will need to be diligently evaluated on a case by case basis. The general idea, when possible, is to consider the Bundle as a single interaction with the enquirer.

Key things to consider:

- Are Privacy Identifiers available and acceptable for all applicants named in the bundle?
- (Principal and/or Dependent).
- Is the Enquirer authorized to take action and/or access the necessary personal information on the file(s) mentioned?
# If YEs → Continue with the necessary actions and reply as a Bundle. See Responding

to a Bundle section for more information.

If No → First verify the Privacy Scenarios below. If none apply, reply with Missing Identifiers/Unauthorized enquirer as needed.

# How to Evaluate a Bundle — Alpha & eGEM

1. Evaluate each Webform's Applicant and Enquirer information to perform the necessary privacy verifications.
## 2. Verify the following:

a. Are all Webforms in the bundle for the same client?
b. If they are for different clients, are the clients listed all part of the same application?
c. Is the Enquirer authorized to obtain any information on file?

#### Responding to a Bundle

You must formulate a reply using existing preformatted texts according to current procedures. See Webcart for more information.

Important Note for Bundles: eGEM-you cannot use the Quick Reply functions to send a reply. O

Alpha-you cannot transfer to a batch reply folder. (i.e. Documents Attached, File status No action required, etc.)

#### Bundles Introduction add-on

You must add the Introduction – Bundling emails (designated agents) text as the first preformatted text in your response:

Current default template →Good day [enquirer name],

### → Thank you for contacting Immigration, Refugees

and Citizenship Canada (IRCC). Add Introduction – Bundling emails text here → Please note that this is a response to the enquiries received below. We verified the information you provided ... Remaining applicable texts here →

# How to unbundle in eGEM

There may be instances when you will need to separate one or multiple emails from a Bundle (ie. uploading documents). Consult the system name below for specific instructions.

**Note: If the Bundle contains enquiries for different clients that are not part of the same

# application, you will always unbundle the Webforms and process them separately***

1. Navigate to the email you would like to separate and click the Remove From Bundle option.

Guidance on Attaching (Un)solicited Documents

## A Bundle may contain one or several Webforms with attachments. However, you may

first need to unbundle one or multiple Webform(s) containing attachment(s) in the following scenarios:

- The Bundle is greater than 5 MB GCMS will not allow you to create an Incoming Correspondence
### containing attachments greater than 5 megabytes (5MB).

- Only one or some of the Webforms included in the Bundle contain attachments.
- Only the Email Message file (original/first Webform sent) needs to be uploaded.
- The Bundle contains Webforms for different applications (same client)
Once you have consulted the list above, follow this job aid to upload the necessary attachments.

eGEM

A. All Webforms include documents to attach in one application:
1. Select the Download All option
## 2. Upload all Webforms to the system.

B. Only one or some of the emails need to be attached: oDownload only the required Webforms and upload as needed.
# Alpha

1. Unbundle all Webforms
2. Upload only those that contain the document(s) that need to be attached.

Change of Contact Information-Guidance for Bundles Clients may send IRCC Webforms to ask about various topics or actions to take, including the notification of a change of contact information.

*Note: for general change of contact information please see our existing procedures.*

## In-Canada applications

A) New phone number and/or email address only:
Please update the file(s) according to the following job aids: phone/email address.

B) An updated address from outside Canada/USA:
1. If applicable, complete all other actions on file;
2. Send a complete reply to client with applicable texts and ensure to confirm the
address change was transferred to the responsible office (see preformatted text: Contact information confirmed or updated).

Y In eGEM – once you have responded:

1. Unbundle the relevant Webform
2. Transfer it to the following:
- LoB: Transfer to Partner
- Sub-LoB: OSC – Change of Address
Outside Canada applications-If the enquirer is providing an updated address, email address and/or telephone number ensure to include the text entitled “Please contact visa office” and add a clarification about the change of contact information.

For all other cases, please update the file(s) according to the corresponding job aid.

Duplicate Webforms You may receive bundles of emails that include exact duplicate enquiries.

If so, no need to confirm reception of duplicate information as the bundles introduction will confirm that detail. Proceed by answering the enquirer's question(s)/request(s).

## Appendix 1 — Privacy Scenarios

Scenario 1: Unidentified Enquirer (Representative) Some representatives do not identify themselves as the “Enquirer” but rather put the name of the client in both the Applicant and Enquirer sections of each Webform.

This situation will create a bundle containing many Webforms from various clients. In this situation, we do not take action, attach documents or provide any case- specific information in cases such as these.

Sample for reference only

### Email 1

Applicant Information Name of applicant: Client 1 Email address: rep@repemail.com DOB of applicant: 1901-01-01 Country of birth: Earthistan

### [...]

Enquirer Information Name of enquirer: Client 1 Relationship to applicant: Applicant Email address: rep@repemail.com

### [...]

Email 2 Applicant Information Name of applicant: Client 2 Email address: rep@repemail.com DOB of applicant: 1899-02-02 Country of birth: Moontopia

### [...] Enquirer Information

Name of enquirer: Client 2 Relationship to applicant: Applicant Email address: rep@repemail.com

## [...]

# ***Reminder-we do not take action, attach documents or provide any information**

eGEM

1.Unbundle relevant Webforms, and select “No, keep it”,
2. Once the Webform is back in your inbox, respond to each unbundled Webform by selecting the “Unidentified Enquirer" text from the Quick Reply option.
3. Code emails as xx20.
4. Click “Send and Process”. Repeat for all unbundled Webforms.
5.
### Alpha

1. Take note of the dates of the oldest and most recent Webforms in the Bundle,
2. Unbundle all relevant emails,
3. Respond to individual Webforms with the Unidentified Enquirer text found in Appendix 2.

## Appendix 2 — Unidentified Enquirer (Representative)

Use the following text according to Scenario 1: Unidentified Enquirer (Representative)**

## Sample below

Current default template →Good day [enquirer name], Thank you for contacting Immigration, Refugees and Citizenship → Canada (IRCC)

Add Appendix 1 text here →[copy/paste text found below]

We have verified the information provided in the Webforms submitted from Month Day, YeartoMonth Day, Year, and regret to inform you that we are unable to process your enquiry nor attach any of the associated documents (if applicable).

The Privacy Act ensures that a person's identity and personal information are protected. The email address provided in the Webforms is attached to numerous individuals therefore, we have concluded that we are not communicating with the concerned clients.

In order to respond to your enquiry, each client must provide their written consent to IRCC by completing the Use of a Representative form and submitting it via the IRCC Webform. You may also contact the appropriate governing body to verify that you are a member in good standing.

Please make sure to enter your client's information under the Applicant Information section and your information under the Enquirer Information section.

## A few useful tips

- If your request is for an outside Canada application, we invite you to contact the responsible Canadian Visa office directly in order to add a representative or designated individual.
- A separate form is required for each individual you wish to represent, even if they are part of the same application.
- If you already provided a Use of a Representative form, we may have not received it properly. Please resend the form and make sure to fill out all sections of the form
correctly.

- If needed, more information on immigration and citizenship representatives is available for you on our website.

1. Toggle Inbox: a view of your “inbox” of all the Webforms currently in your Alpha folder.
2. Fetch multiple emails: allows you to fetch multiple Webforms at a time.
3. Load emails: allows you to fetch one Webform at a time.
4. EN: open an email draft in English.
5. FR: open an email draft in French.
6. Code: a complete list of wrap up codes from which you can select the appropriate code for your email.
7. Hold: allows for an email to be placed on or removed form hold it may be processed (to be used when awaiting guidance or Quality approval).
8. Transfer: allows you to transfer or copy a message to another LOB or back to Question Box (Inventory).
9. Reject: allows you to reject an email (should only be done if advised by procedure).
000254

mmigration, Réfugiés Information disclosed under the Access to Information Act L'information divulguée en vertu de la [oi sur l'accès à Finformatiot

# 10. Process: to be used once all necessary actions are taken (as per procedure).

11. Quick reply: access scripts.

12. Options: access and modify Alpha parameters & also to submit an exception.

13. Script assistant: - NULL -
# 14. Attachments: list of attachments included in the clients' Webform. You can copy

the list of the name of the attachments by clicking on the word “Attachments”.

15. Auto Copy: allows you to copy information from the Webform (UCl, File Number, PA Name, Enquirer Name, etc.).

16. Audit Log: Chronological record of actions/activities since fetching the Webform.

17. HighRiskLoB: signals that the Webform is part of a Contentious LoB.

18. Priority Keyword: flagged with a priority keyword implemented due to an urgent/crisis situation (example: Iran2023).

19. Notes: when in green it means that it may contain important notes on how to proceed.
# 20. History: an audit trail of the Webform's history.

# Alpha Best Practices

1. Ensure settings are set to fetch no more than 10 Webforms at a time.

2. When possible, fetch more than 1 email at a time.

3. When possible, do not have any other Excel-based programs open.

4. When possible, do not leave any Webforms in your Alpha at the end of your shift and advise your TL if ever Webforms should be removed.

What is a Grouped Webform? Used as an inventory reduction strategy, Webforms sent by the same email address (enquirer) are grouped together so that the enquirer in question receives one (1) response to all enquiries they have sent over a certain period.

The group of emails is also referred to as a “Bundle”.

In Alpha, Bundles will look like this (image below shows a group of 2):

## What is a True Bundle?

A “True Bundle” is when all Webforms contained in the Bundle have matching Applicant & Enquirer identifiers (see Sample Identifier 1&2 below).

# Breaking Down Webforms

#### Summarizing-How to Identify the Enquirer's Needs

When reading a Webform, you are essentially trying to understand the Enquirer's needs based on the story they are telling.

Who are the Applicant(s) and Enquirer? Main Character(s)

## Where is the application being processed? Inside or

# Setting

outside Canada? Which LoB(s) is/are involved? Plot Conflict What is the question, concern, and/or request?

#### Possible main characters:

Principal Applicant (PA) Sponsor (SPR) Dependent (DEP) Parent/Legal Guardian Representative (REP) Unauthorized Third Party

### Researching & Transmitting Information-Effective Debrief

When reviewing Webforms, as well as when contacting colleagues for support, we should always be mindful of the way we research and present information.

Be precise – it is important to be accurate when gathering information.

Analyse the request as well as the application and gather information accurately.

## Research the information in the appropriate system/guide/page (program

information, Webcart, Connexion, etc.). Be prepared to provide relevant information.

Be concise – present details in short, direct sentences and ensure to give information about: Y “The authorized rep for the PA is calling about the..."

### Main Characters:

V“...in-Canada..."

## Setting:

V“...work permit extension..."
Plot: V “...that was not received after 6 weeks."

## Conflict:

ESU Training – Participant Guide v3 Prepared by M. de Lima

000450

Immigration, Refugees and Citizenship Canedaet Citoyenneté Canada L'nformation divulguée en vertu de la toi sur l’accès à Tinformation

Be clear – state what you have verified and what you need clarification on.

Ex: "I've verified Correspondence Not Received but I don't think I have the right procedure. Can you help me identify the correct procedure and its text?"

Contacting ESU Mentors After you've analyzed the Webform and the application quoted therein, you should only contact ESU Mentors when:

■You need guidance as to which ESU procedure to use. You need clarification on an ESU procedure. You need help modifying a text. You need help with text assembly.

*Important Notes* ESU Mentors cannot advise on or clarify Privacy. You must contact PSU. If you are experiencing issues with tools/systems after completing the necessary troubleshooting steps or you require guidance on tools/systems, please reach out to your respective TL

# Contacting PSU

## After you've analyzed the Webform and the application quoted therein, you should only

contact PSU when:

You have a program question. You need clarification on program information. You need clarification on a CHD procedure. You have questions or need clarification on Privacy.

*Important Notes* PSU cannot provide guidance on which text to use to respond to a Webform. You must reach out to an ESU Mentor.

If PSU is unable to provide guidance on program information, a CHD procedure, or privacy, take note of the name of the PSU agent as well as the interaction ID and contact an ESU Mentor.

Question 1

## Name the two (2) RPA strategies currently being used by ESU

Answer 1 Pre-Agent Bot Action & Post-Agent Bot Action

# Question 2

True or False: RPA answers all questions submitted via Webform Answer 2 False – RPA is used to upload attachments provided via Webform only. It does not respond to additional enquiries.

Question 3 Name 5 of the 7 criteria that must be met to be eligible for RPA

Answer 3

1. Application number: must appear in the File Number field of the Webform. The following formats can be accepted:
- F000123123 - INLAND SCLP;
- XS00000987 (Prospective applications-RPA will very both with and without the “X”).
2. Privacy must be met.
3. Unsolicited documents.
4. Solicited documents for which no line has been created in Correspondence-Incoming view.
5. Open/ongoing IR application
000577

Immigration, RefugeesImmigration, Réfugiés and Citizenship Canadaet Citoyenneté Canada L'nformation divulguée en vertu de la toi sur l’accès à Tinformation

6. In-Canada applications For closed applications: the document should only be attached if it brings value
i.e., Medical exam completed after application was closed.
7. Webforms received in Technical format as long as:
- The application number must be indicated in the correct field.
- The link leads to an unsolicited document
# Question 4

True or False: If an enquirer submits documents for a closed IR application, it is still eligible for RPA.

Answer 4 False – closed IR applications are not eligible for RPA. You must respond with “Please Contact Visa Office"

Question 5

# In the Pre-Agent strategy, RPA uploads the attachments prior to you receiving the

Webform for processing.

How will you know if RPA was successful? Provide details

How will you know if RPA has failed? Provide details.

Answer 5 If RPA is successful, a banner will appear stating: Pre-Agent Bot Action "Upload to GCMS” is successful.

## If RPA fails, a banner will appear stating: Pre-Agent Bot Action wasn't able to complete

its task, you may need to manually do this work prior to wrap-up of this request.

Question 6 Pre-Agent strategy-True or False: If a Webform contains attachments AND additional questions, you should only respond with information pertaining to the additional questions and NOT confirm reception of the attachments.

Answer 6 True – since the auto-reply sent by the RPA already confirms reception of the attachments, you only respond to the additional questions the enquirer has.

Question 7 Name three things RPA fails to verify that agents must verify on each Webform regardless of the RPA strategy implemented.

# Answer 7 1. Privacy

### 2. Authorization

3. File Number
Question 8 In your own words, explain the 5 steps agents must stake as part of their role in the Pre-Agent strategy.

Answer 8

## 1. Verify privacy is met

2. Ensure the document was uploaded to the correct application
3. Ensure the document eligible for RPA or if it must be processed manually?
4. Is the enquirer authorized?
5. Are there additional questions or requests?
Question 9 Post-Agent strategy-True or False: If a Webform contains attachments AND additional questions, you should only respond with information pertaining to the additional questions and NOT confirm reception of the attachments.

Answer 9 False – you will answer with the text “Post-Agent RPA – Documents Received” (which confirms reception of the attachments) + any other applicable texts to provide a complete response.

Question 10 In your own words, describe the difference between the Pre-Agent and Post-Agent strategies.

### Answer 10

#### In the Pre-Agent strategy, RPA will scan the Webform for any attachments and an

#### application number prior to the Webform being received by the ESU agent.

#### In the Post-Agent strategy, the Webform is received by the ESU agent and then

Only the PowerPoint will be reviewed. If participants would like further information, they

vetted to see if it is RPA eligible.

# Question 1

If a client/enquirer is providing several documents in the same Webform what criteria needs to be met top be able to attach documents? Provide the path and section where this information can be found

Answer 1 You can attach them only if they are:

- all for the same client;
- for different clients who share the same application number; or
- for different clients who have different application numbers AND have the same Group # in GCMS:
- if yes: follow the appropriate procedure below.
- if no and you determine that the client/requester is providing documents for different clients who do not share the same file: reply to them using the text Documents provided for more than one client.
000591

Immigration, Refugees Immigration, Réfugiés and Citizenship Canedaet Citoyenneté Canada Information disclosed under the Accessto Information Act

Path: Procedures > Email Support Unit > Procedures-Documents (un)solicited received from clients > Before Proceeding

Question 2 True or False? You cannot attach documents if the Enquirer is unauthorized. Provide the path and section where this information can be found

Answer 2 False. You can attach any document provided by unauthorized third parties (except for an LMIA/CAQ) Path: Procedures > Email Support Unit > Procedures-Documents (un)solicited received from clients > Before Proceeding

Question 3 What is the difference between a solicited and unsolicited document? Provide the path and section(s) where this information can be found

Answer 3 A solicited document is a document that has been requested by an IRCC office for which a line was created in the Correspondence-Incoming view; If a line was not created in the Correspondence-Incoming view in GCMS to allow the client to upload this information, it will be considered as an unsolicited document. Path: Procedures > Email Support Unit > Procedures-Documents (un)solicited received from clients > Document Solicited

An unsolicited document is a document or information that has not been requested by an IRCC office and that the client is sending as an additional document in support of their application; If a line was not created in the Correspondence-Incoming view in GCMS to allow the client to upload this information, then it will be considered as an unsolicited document. Path: Procedures > Email Support Unit > Procedures-Documents (un)solicited received from clients > Document Unsolicited

Answer 4

Question 6 You verify a file and realize that another agent processed and responded to the Enquirer the same day. Which text would you send? Provide the path where this information can be found.

Answer 6 If the enquiry is processed the same day that the action was taken: initial duplicate request: reply to client using the text Duplicate request. subsequent duplicate requests processed the same day: reject the email.

Path: Procedures > Email Support Unit > Procedures-Processing Emails > Duplicate request or action already taken

Answer 7 Action already taken-Less than 60 days Path: Procedures > Email Support Unit > Procedures-Processing Emails > Duplicate request or action already taken

Answer 8 Action already taken – IR

Question 9

# When should you follow the Upload Mix-Up procedure? Provide the path where this

information can be found.

Answer 9 When you erroneously uploaded a client's document(s) to another client's file or notice a client's information uploaded in another client's file.

Path: Procedures > Email Support Unit > Procedures-Processing Emails > Upload mix-up

Question 10 What steps should you take if you've upload both a correct and incorrect Webform to a file?

Answer 10 If both a correct and an incorrect Web form were uploaded in an Incoming Correspondence: create a new Incoming Correspondence with only the correct Web form;

- Process the Web form that was previously uploaded in error by: (if applicable), uploading it in the right file; and/or following the appropriate ESU procedure if the incorrect procedure was
followed;

- Send the referral, Upload mix-up, including your team leader in Cc.
Path: Procedures > Email Support Unit > Procedures-Processing Emails > Upload mix-up

### Question 11

Once you've ensured privacy is met, what is important to verify prior to attaching a Webform to a client's file?

Answer 11 Verify if the document was previously added to file prior to attaching it.

# Question 12

True or False? You notice that a client's LMIA was added to their PR application instead of their WP application. This is an Upload Mix-Up.

000594

Immigration, Refugees and Citizenship Canedaet Citoyenneté Canada Information disclosed under the Accessto Information Act

Answer 12 False. Upload Mix-Up should only be followed if there is a chance of a privacy breach. In this scenario, the expectation is that you change the IC status to cancelled and upload the LMIA to the correct WP application.

Answer 14 Processing a Returned Incoming Correspondence or Referral Path: Procedures > Email Support Unit > Procedures-Processing a Returned Incoming Correspondence or Referral

# Question 15

True or False When processing a returned referral, you can simply transfer the referral and do not need to compose a new one.

Answer 15 False-If your referral needs to be re-sent, it is important to compose a new referral (instead of just transferring it). This will ensure that CE CSC Operations / Opérations CSC EC (IRCC) is automatically in Cc and that there is a record of the referral having been re-sent.

Path: Procedures > Email Support Unit > Procedures-Processing a Returned Incoming Correspondence or Referral > An action is required-For referrals

# Grouped Webform – Change of Contact

## Information

Clients may send IRCC Webforms to ask about various topics or actions to take, including the notification of a change of contact information.

### In-Canada applications

A. New phone number and/or email B. An updated address from address only.outside Canada or USA. Please update the file(s) according to the 1. If applicable, complete all other actions following Change of Contact Information on file. procedure.
2. Send a complete reply to client with applicable texts and ensure to confirm the address change was transferred to the responsible office
3. Once you have responded, unbundle the relevant Webform and transfer it to the following LOB: Transfer to Partner > OSC – Change of Address
000736

# Grouped Webform – Change of Contact

## Information

Clients may send IRCC Webforms to ask about various topics or actions to take, including the notification of a change of contact information.

Outside-Canada applications

If the enquirer is providing an updated address, email address and/or telephone number ensure to include the text entitled “Please contact visa office" and add a clarification about the change of contact information.

For all other cases

Please update the file(s) according to the corresponding job aid.

000737

## Grouped Webform – Duplicate Webforms

You may receive Bundles that include exact duplicate enquiries.

If so, there is no need to confirm reception of duplicate information as the Bundles Introduction text will confirm that detail.

Proceed by answering the enquirer's question(s)/request(s).

# What is a Webform?

A Webform allows clients to communicate with IRCC via our website.

## IRCC web form: Contact us online

1 0

## If you're affected by the situation in Ukraine

Learn about our response to the situation in Ukraine, including

- the immigration measures we've put in place
- how to send a priority request if you need to contact us
### Submitting an enquiry

Even if you haven't heard from us recently, we're still processing your application. You don't need to do anything else. If we need more information, we'll contact you. Our agents do not have access to more information about your case status or processing time than you can access online. Before contacting us, you can

- check your application status online find answers to your questions in our Help Centre
- get general information on our website
We understand that timely access to client support is important. Thank you for your patience. 000832

# REMINDER FOR INITIAL PR CARDS

In the case of newly arrived PRs or in-Canada PR grant, the following parties are authorized to take necessary action on the initial PR Card:

Y the Principle Applicant (PA) Y Dependent family members sharing the same immigration file number and who immigrated together Y Their authorized representatives

ATIP > Frequently Asked Questions > Principal Applicant and Family Members Immigrated Together

## This does not apply to Sponsors acting on behalf of the PA.

To follow up on the initial PRC, they must be authorized in the Sponsorship application or the PRC application. 000881

# What's the Story-Summarizing

When reading a Webform, you are essentially trying to understand the Enquirer's needs based on the story they are telling.

Main character(s): Who are the Applicant(s) and Enquirer? Where is the application being processed? Inside or Setting: outside Canada? Which LoB(s) is involved? Plot: What is the question, concern and/or request? Conflict 000922

Representative Principal Applicant (PA)

Parent or Legal Guardian Sponsor (SPR)

Dependent (DEP) Unauthorized Third Party 000923

## Activity 3 - Privacy & Incomplete Authorization

(60 minutes)

Instructions for Trainers Advise participants that: all answers can be found in WebCART and/or the Participant Guide;

# They will be provided 30 minutes to complete and send the activity to

their respective TLs with panelists in Cc. Ask participants to raise their virtual hand once they have finished; Remind participants who panelists are.

All answers can be found in WebCart. They been provided below and include: Text names Instructions or information to provide to participants WebCart Pathways Q1 In your own words, explain the difference between Privacy and Authorization.

Answer

- Privacy: requires that the client be adequately identified by providing the required identifiers before any personal information is released.
- Authorization: the action or act of giving someone legal permission to access the file(s). The person authorized MUST still meet privacy by providing the required identifiers for the client in question.

The definitions as written above do not exist in WebCART. Simply ensure that participants understand the difference between both and that although Privacy may be met, the third party would need authorization in most circumstances. the path

The UCl, application number and country of birth are all case-specific pieces Q3 of information that can be used to pass privacy.

Name three (3) others and provide the path in Webcart where the information can be found.

Answer immigration document number; type of application submitted; date the application was submitted; date of initial admission in Canada (month and year); current immigration status; educational institution; date permanent resident status was obtained; passport number; the office where the application was submitted; IPRMS receipt number;

number of persons included in the application; family members' names; employer's name; the amount paid for the application; etc.

# Q5True or False-a parent/legal guardian is able to access their adult child's

application for sponsorship as long as the application began prior to their 18th or 19th birthday.

Provide an explanation as well as a path to the information in Webcart.

## Answer

True. As long as the (IMM or CTZ) application process was started prior to the child turning 18/19 years of age, parents/legal guardians still have access and can act on behalf of their child after the child has turned 18 or 19. However, if the minor who turned 18 or 19 no longer wants their parents/legal guardians to access their file, they must send a request to the IRCC office that is responsible for their file advising them of that fact.

Program Information > Tools and Best Practices > Privacy & Access To Information Acts > Frequently Asked Questions > Minors who turned 18 or 19 years old

## Q6For sponsorship applications, is the Sponsor authorized to obtain information

about the PA's application for Sponsorship? Provide the path in Webcart.

# Answer

No. They require authorization to access or act on behalf of each other. However, they can access their own personal information that may be found in the other's file or obtain information that is considered shared.

Q8True or False – Authorization is considered complete if the representative does not to specify which party they are representing (SPR, PA, DEP, etc.).

Answer False-The note/information is considered incomplete if one or more of the following applies: (If the application is for more than one applicant) The IMM 5476/5475/Power of attorney does not specify if the authorization is for the sponsor (SPR), principal applicant (PA) and/or dependent child (DEP) (in GCMS, the Other Relationship Descrip field is empty);

Path: Procedures > CHD > General > Authorization-Not on File/Incomplete > Before Proceeding

Q1In your own words, describe what a Bundle or Grouped Webform is.

Answer Webforms sent by the same email address (Enquirer) are grouped together so that the enquirer in question receives one (1) response to all enquiries they have sent over a certain period.

This information was presented in the PowerPoint and can be found on page 59 of the Participant Guide.

Q4At times, representatives do not identify themselves as the “Enquirer" but rather put the name of the client in both the Applicant and Enquirer sections of each Webform. This creates a bundle with various clients. Which action(s) should you take if any? Which text(s) should you send?

Answer We DO NOT take action, attach documents or provide any information. We send “Unidentified Enquirer” text.

This information was presented in the PowerPoint and can be found on page 71 Of the Participant Guide.

Q5True or False – When reviewing a Bundle with various family member with the same email address, it is ok to respond as a Bundle even if they are not part of the same application.

Answer False. We must first unbundle and then we can action each Webform individually.

This information was presented in the PowerPoint and can be found on page 73 Of the Participant Guide.

NH CIT-PRC – ESU Training – Instructor Guide v1 Prepared by M. de Lima 001001

Immigration, Refugees and Citizenship Canada et Ciloyenneé Canada Information disclosed under the Access to Information Act Lnomdn

Q6 List the steps to take when processing a Bundle in eGEM.

Answer

1. Evaluate each Webform's Applicant & Enquirer sections and perform the necessary privacy verification.
2. Verify the following:
a. Are all Webforms are for the same client?
b. If there are different clients, are they all part of the same application?
3. Once privacy/authorization are verified:
a. Upload documents or take necessary action (if applicable)
b. Draft a response containing information that the authorization provides for.
Q7True or False – You can use the Quick Reply option to respond to Bundles.

Answer

# False

Answer Introduction – Bundling emails (designated agents)

Q9List the steps to unbundle in eGEM.

Answer

1. Navigate to the email you would like to separate and click the Remove From Bundle option.
NH CIT-PRC – ESU Training – Instructor Guide v1Prepared by M. de Lima 001002

Immigration, Refugees and Citizenshi Canada et Ciloyeneé Canada Information disclosed under the Access to Information Act Lnomdn

2. A window will appear. Select one of the following options:
- Yes, return it: This option returns the Webform to inventory. No, keep it: this option return the Webform to your inbox for further processing and/or transfer.
Q10 List the steps to transfer a Bundle in eGEM

Answer

1. Verify if only certain Webforms must be transferred or the entire bundle
2. Unbundle all applicable Webforms
3. Make sure you choose “No, keep it”
4. Update the LoB on the Email Request page
5. Select transfer

Q7Enquiry: We are Counsel for Mr. Sean Paul, whose application for permanent residence (#E003456789) through the Express Entry was rejected on 2023/06/15. We hereby ask you to reconsider your decision and to re-open the application. The reason for the rejection appears to be that his Trinidad& Tobago police certificate was issued on 2021/01/20 and his last residence in this country was 2021/02/14. Please be advised that Mr. Paul left Trinidad on 2021/01/20 and has never gone back to this country. He went to Jamaica on that day and stayed in Jamaica until 2021/02/15 when he came to Canada. Hid trip to Jamaica was noted in his travel history. As he did not have a fixed address in Jamaica, he put his last address in Trinidad for the period of time he stayed in Jamaica. Between 2021/01/20 and 2021/02/14, he was physically living in Jamaica, not in Trinidad. In our submission, we did explain that he has never gone back to Trinidad after his police certificate was issued. Please note this is the 2nd time Mr. Sean Paul was rejected a PR application and it has caused considerable stress to him. We apologize for the confusion about his address history and sincerely hope you will reopen his application. Copies of his Express Entry address/travel history and our explanation letter attached to the submitted Trinidad police certificate are all attached for your reference. Thank you very much for your reconsideration.

Answer The representative is submitting a request for reconsideration and is providing more details concerning the clients travel.

Q1 If a client/enquirer is providing several documents in the same Webform what criteria needs to be met to be able to attach documents? Provide the path and

##### Prepared by M. de Lima

Immigration, RefugeesImmigration, Réfugiés and Citizenship Canedaot Citoyenneté Canada L'nformation divulguée en vertu de la toi sur l’accès à Tinformation

Answer You can attach them only if they are:

- all for the same client;
- for different clients who share the same application number; or for different clients who have different application numbers AND have the same Group # in GCMS: If yes follow the appropriate procedure below. oIf no and you determine that the client/requester is providing documents for different clients who do not share the same file: reply to them using the text Documents provided for more than one client.
Procedures > Email Support Unit > Procedures-Documents (un)solicited received from clients > Before Proceeding

Q2 True or False – You cannot attach documents if the enquirer is unauthorized. Provide the path and section where this information can be found

Answer False. You can attach any document provided by unauthorized third parties (except for an LMIA/CAQ)

Procedures > Email Support Unit > Procedures-Documents (un)solicited received from clients > Before Proceeding

Q3 What is the difference between a solicited and unsolicited document? Provide the path and section(s) where this information can be found

Answer A solicited document is a document that has been requested by an IRCC office for which a line was created in the Correspondence-Incoming view; If a line was not created in the Correspondence-Incoming view in GCMS to allow the client to upload this information, it will be considered as an unsolicited document.

NH CIT-PRC – ESU Training – Instructor Guide v1 Prepared by M. de Lima 001014

Immigration, Refugees and Citizenship Canadaot Citoyenneté Canada L'nformation divulguée en vertu de la toi sur l’accès à Tinformation

Procedures > Email Support Unit > Procedures-Documents (un)solicited received from clients > Document Solicited

An unsolicited document is a document or information that has not been requested by an IRCC office and that the client is sending as an additional document in support of their application; If a line was not created in the Correspondence-Incoming view in GCMS to allow the client to upload this information, then it will be considered as an unsolicited document. Procedures > Email Support Unit > Procedures-Documents (un)solicited received from clients > Document Unsolicited

Q4 Which type of document requires you add a document number when attaching to file? Provide the path where this information can be found?

# Answer

Passport Email Support Unit > Job aid-Creating an incoming correspondence and adding an attachment > Add an attachment

Procedures > Email Support Unit > Job aid-Creating an incoming correspondence and adding an attachment

Q6 You verify a file and realize that another agent processed and responded to the Enquirer the same day. Which text would you send? Provide the path where this information can be found.

Answer If the enquiry is processed the same day that the action was taken:

- Initial duplicate request – reply to client using the text Duplicate request. Subsequent duplicate requests processed the same day – reject the email.
Procedures > Email Support Unit > Procedures-Processing Emails > Duplicate request or action already taken

Q7 You review a file and realize that another agent processed the enquirers request 45 days prior. Which text would you send?

Answer Action already taken-Less than 60 days

Procedures > Email Support Unit > Procedures-Processing Emails > Duplicate request or action already taken

Q8 The Enquirer is providing a document that has already been added to file 2 weeks prior. The file is being processed by New Delhi. Which text would you send?

Answer Action already taken – IR Procedures > Email Support Unit > Procedures-Processing Emails > Duplicate request or action already taken

NH CIT-PRC – ESU Training – Instructor Guide v1 Prepared by M. de Lima 001016

Immigration, Refugees and Citizenship Canadaot Citoyenneté Canada Information disclosed under the Access to Information Act L'nformation divulguée en vertu de la toi sur l’accès à Tinformation

Q9When should you follow the Upload Mix-Up procedure? Provide the path where this information can be found.

Answer When you erroneously uploaded a client's document(s) to another client's file or notice a client's information uploaded in another client's file.

Procedures > Email Support Unit > Procedures-Processing Emails > Upload mix-up

Q10 What steps should you take if you've upload both a correct and incorrect Webform to a file?

Answer If both a correct and an incorrect Web form were uploaded in an Incoming Correspondence:

- Create a new Incoming Correspondence with only the correct Web form; Process the Web form that was previously uploaded in error by: (if applicable), uploading it in the right file; and/or following the appropriate ESU procedure if the incorrect procedure was followed; Send the referral, Upload mix-up, including your team leader in Cc.
Procedures > Email Support Unit > Procedures-Processing Emails > Upload mix-up

Q11 Once you've ensured privacy is met, what is important to verify prior to attaching a Webform to a client's file?

Answer Verify if the document was previously added to file prior to attaching it.

Q12True or False — You notice that a client's LMIA was added to their PR application instead of their WP application. This is an Upload Mix-Up.

Answer

### False. Upload Mix-Up should only be followed if there is a chance of a privacy

breach. In this scenario, the expectation is that you change the IC status to cancelled and upload the LMIA to the correct WP application.

## Text names

Instructions or information to provide to participants WebCart Pathways Q1 In your own words, explain the difference between Privacy and Authorization.

Answer

## Privacy: requires that the client be adequately identified by providing

the required identifiers before any personal information is released.

- Authorization: the action or act of giving someone legal permission to access the file(s). The person authorized MUST still meet privacy by providing the required identifiers for the client in question.
The definitions as written above do not exist in WebCART. Simply ensure that participants understand the difference between both and that although Privacy may be met, the third party would need authorization in most circumstances.

the path

Full name DOB One case-specific piece of information

## of information that can be used to pass privacy.

number of persons included in the application; family members' names; employer's name; the amount paid for the application; etc.

# True or False – a parent/legal guardian is able to access their adult child's

## application for sponsorship as long as the application began prior to their 18th

Provide an explanation as well as a path to the information in Webcart.

### Prepared by M. de Lima

mmigration, Réfugiés and Citizenship Canadaet Citoyenneté Canada Information disclosed under the Access to Information Act Lnfomdn

## True. As long as the (IMM or CTZ) application process was started prior to the

child turning 18/19 years of age, parents/legal guardians still have access and can act on behalf of their child after the child has turned 18 or 19. However, if

## the minor who turned 18 or 19 no longer wants their parents/legal guardians to

access their file, they must send a request to the IRCC office that is responsible for their file advising them of that fact.

Program Information > Tools and Best Practices > Privacy & Access To Information Acts > Frequently Asked Questions > Minors who turned 18 or 19 years old

# Q6For sponsorship applications, is the Sponsor authorized to obtain information

about the PA's application for Sponsorship? Provide the path in Webcart.

Answer

## No. They require authorization to access or act on behalf of each other.

However, they can access their own personal information that may be found in the other's file or obtain information that is considered shared

Program Information > Tools and Best Practices > Privacy & Access To Information Acts > Frequently Asked Questions > Access to Each Other's File

## Q7 For authorization on file to be considered complete, the representative's name

must appear in which screens/views? Provide the path to the information in

## Webcart.

Answer IMM/CIT screen > Clients & Parties view; Clients screen > Notes view (Office must not show as GCMS-System)

Procedures > CHD > General > Authorization-Not on File/Incomplete > Before Proceeding

## Q8True or False – Authorization is considered complete if the representative

does not to specify which party they are representing (SPR, PA, DEP, etc.).

Answer

## False-The note/information is considered incomplete if one or more of

the following applies: (If the application is for more than one applicant) The IMM 5476/5475/Power of attorney does not specify if the authorization is for the sponsor (SPR), principal applicant (PA) and/or dependent child (DEP) (in

## GCMS, the Other Relationship Descrip field is empty);

Path: Procedures > CHD > General > Authorization-Not on File/Incomplete > Before Proceeding

Q1Name the two (2) RPA strategies currently being used by ESu

Answer Pre-Agent Bot Action & Post-Agent Bot Action

Q2 True or False – RPA answers all questions submitted via Webform.

Answer False – RPA is used to upload attachments provided via Webform only. It does not respond to additional enquiries.

Q3Name 5 of the 7 criteria that must be met to be eligible for RPA

Answer

1. Application number: must appear in the File Number field of the Webform. The following formats can be accepted: F000123123 - INLAND SCLP; XS00000987 (Prospective applications-RPA will very both with and without the “X”).
2. Privacy must be met.
3. Unsolicited documents.
4. Solicited documents for which no line has been created in Correspondence-Incoming view.
5. Open/ongoing IR application
6. In-Canada applications For closed applications: the document should only be attached if it brings value
i.e., Medical exam completed after application was closed.
8. Webforms received in Technical format as long as: The application number must be indicated in the correct field. The link leads to an unsolicited document.
Q4 True or False – If an enquirer submits documents for a closed IR application, it is still eligible for RPA.

Answer False – closed IR applications are not eligible for RPA. You must respond with “Please Contact Visa Office”

Q5In the Pre-Agent strategy, RPA uploads the attachments prior to you receiving the Webform for processing.

### Q10 In your own words, describe the difference between the Pre-Agent and Post-

Agent strategies.

Answer

### Pre-Agent Strategy: RPA will scan the Webform for any attachments and an

application number prior to the Webform being received by the ESU agent.

Post-Agent Strategy: the Webform is received by the ESU agent and then vetted to see if it is RPA eligible.

Q1In your own words, describe what a Bundle or Grouped Webform is.

Answer Webforms sent by the same email address (Enquirer) are grouped together so that the enquirer in question receives one (1) response to all enquiries they have sent over a certain period.

This information was presented in the PowerPoint and can be found on page 59 of the Participant Guide.

Q5True or False – When reviewing a Bundle with various family member with the same email address, it is ok to respond as a Bundle even if they are not part of the same application.

# Q6List the steps to take when processing a Bundle in eGEM.

Answer

1. Evaluate each Webform's Applicant & Enquirer sections and perform the necessary privacy verification.
2. Verify the following:
a. Are all Webforms are for the same client?
b. If there are different clients, are they all part of the same application?
3. Once privacy/authorization are verified:
a. Upload documents or take necessary action (if applicable)
b. Draft a response containing information that the authorization provides for.

Answer

Q9List the steps to unbundle in eGEM.

Answer

1. Navigate to the email you would like to separate and click the Remove From Bundle option.
2. A window will appear. Select one of the following options: Yes, return it: This option returns the Webform to inventory.
- No, keep it: this option return the Webform to your inbox for further processing and/or transfer.
Q10List the steps to transfer a Bundle in eGEM

# Answer

1. Verify if only certain Webforms must be transferred or the entire bundle
2. Unbundle all applicable Webforms
3. Make sure you choose “No, keep it”
4. Update the LoB on the Email Request page
5. Select transfer

# Q6Enquiry:

Hello immigration officer, Earlier this month, I sent a web form asking about a message being displayed in the IRCC portal. The message read "We have requested a medical exam. Check your messages below for details". However, there were no messages in my portal, and no communication sent to the emails listed in our application. Now when I check the portal, I see the following message: "We did not receive the results of your required medical exam. Check your medical exam request message below for details." Once again, we are unable to see the medical exam request or any other details within the portal. Can you please send us the required medical exam to one of the emails listed below (both are also included in our original application): Meghan Markle (principal applicant) email: mmarkle@email.com Henry Charles Aibert David Mountbatten (sponsor/representative) email: prince_not the singer@email.com Please let me know if you have any questions or concerns. We are really worried and want to make sure we are not missing anything due to technical issues within the portal. Any help would be much appreciated. # Q7Enquiry:

We are Counsel for Mr. Sean Paul, whose application for permanent residence

## (#E003456789) through the Express Entry was rejected on 2023/06/15. We

hereby ask you to reconsider your decision and to re-open the application. The reason for the rejection appears to be that his Trinidad& Tobago police certificate was issued on 2021/01/20 and his last residence in this country was 2021/02/14. Please be advised that Mr. Paul left Trinidad on 2021/01/20 and has never gone back to this country. He went to Jamaica on that day and stayed in Jamaica until 2021/02/15 when he came to Canada. Hid trip to Jamaica was noted in his travel history. As he did not have a fixed address in Jamaica, he put his last address in Trinidad for the period of time he stayed in

## Jamaica. Between 2021/01/20 and 2021/02/14, he was physically living in

Jamaica, not in Trinidad. In our submission, we did explain that he has never gone back to Trinidad after his police certificate was issued. Please note this is the 2nd time Mr. Sean Paul was rejected a PR application and it has caused considerable stress to him. We apologize for the confusion about his address history and sincerely hope you will reopen his application. Copies of his Express Entry address/travel history and our explanation letter attached to the submitted

### Trinidad police certificate are all attached for your reference. Thank you very

much for your reconsideration.

Q1 If a client/enquirer is providing several documents in the same Webform what criteria needs to be met to be able to attach documents? Provide the path and section where this information can be found.

Answer

New Hire IMM_ESU Training – Instructor Guide v3 Prepared by M. de Lima

001177

Immigration, Refugees Immigration, Réfugiés and Citizenship Canadaet Citoyenneté Canada Information disclosed under the Access to Information Act L'nformation divulguée en vertu de la toi sur l’accès à Tinformation

You can attach them only if they are:

- all for the same client;
- for different clients who share the same application number; or for different clients who have different application numbers AND have the same Group # in GCMS: If yes follow the appropriate procedure below. If no and you determine that the client/requester is providing
documents for different clients who do not share the same file: reply to them using the text Documents provided for more than one client.

Procedures > Email Support Unit > Procedures-Documents (un)solicited received from clients > Before Proceeding

Q2True or False – You cannot attach documents if the enquirer is unauthorized. Provide the path and section where this information can be found

Answer False. You can attach any document provided by unauthorized third parties (except for an LMIA/CAQ)

Procedures > Email Support Unit > Procedures-Documents (un)solicited received from clients > Before Proceeding

Q3 What is the difference between a solicited and unsolicited document? Provide the path and section(s) where this information can be found

Answer A solicited document is a document that has been requested by an IRCC office for which a line was created in the Correspondence-Incoming view; If a line was not created in the Correspondence-Incoming view in ĠCMS to allow the client to upload this information, it will be considered as an unsolicited document. Procedures > Email Support Unit > Procedures-Documents (un)solicited received from clients > Document Solicited

An unsolicited document is a document or information that has not been requested by an IRCC office and that the client is sending as an additional document in support of their application; If a line was not created in the Correspondence-Incoming view in GCMS to allow the client to upload this information, then it will be considered as an unsolicited document. Procedures > Email Support Unit > Procedures-Documents (un)solicited received from clients > Document Unsolicited

001178

Q4 Which type of document requires you add a document number when attaching to file? Provide the path where this information can be found?

Answer Passport Email Support Unit > Job aid-Creating an incoming correspondence and adding an attachment > Add an attachment

Q5 Fill in the blanks: “(For eGEM users only) It is important tofolder everyday at the _, please follow this job aid."

Answer (For eGEM users only) It is important to clear your Downloads folder everyday at the end of your work day, please follow this job aid.

Procedures > Email Support Unit > Job aid-Creating an incoming correspondence and adding an attachment Q6 You verify a file and realize that another agent processed and responded to the Enquirer the same day. Which text would you send? Provide the path where this information can be found.

Answer If the enquiry is processed the same day that the action was taken:

- Initial duplicate request – reply to client using the text Duplicate request.
- Subsequent duplicate requests processed the same day – reject the email.
Procedures > Email Support Unit > Procedures-Processing Emails > Duplicate request or action already taken

Q7 You review a file and realize that another agent processed the enquirers request 45 days prior. Which text would you send?

Answer Action already taken-Less than 60 days

Procedures > Email Support Unit > Procedures-Processing Emails > Duplicate request or action already taken

Action already taken — IR Procedures > Email Support Unit > Procedures-Processing Emails > Duplicate request or action already taken

Q9 When should you follow the Upload Mix-Up procedure? Provide the path where this information can be found.

Answer When you erroneously uploaded a client's document(s) to another client's file or notice a client's information uploaded in another client's file.

Procedures > Email Support Unit > Procedures-Processing Emails > Upload mix-up

Q10 What steps should you take if you've upload both a correct and incorrect Webform to a file?

Answer If both a correct and an incorrect Web form were uploaded in an Incoming Correspondence: Create a new Incoming Correspondence with only the correct Web form; Process the Web form that was previously uploaded in error by: (if applicable), uploading it in the right file; and/or following the appropriate ESU procedure if the incorrect procedure was followed; Send the referral, Upload mix-up, including your team leader in Cc.

Procedures > Email Support Unit > Procedures-Processing Emails > Upload mix-up

Q11Once you've ensured privacy is met, what is important to verify prior to attaching a Webform to a client's file?

Answer Verify if the document was previously added to file prior to attaching it.

Q12 True or False – You notice that a client's LMIA was added to their PR application instead of their WP application. This is an Upload Mix-Up.

Answer False. Upload Mix-Up should only be followed if there is a chance of a privacy breach. In this scenario, the expectation is that you change the IC status to cancelled and upload the LMIA to the correct WP application.

In your own words, explain the difference between Privacy and Authorization.

Privacy: requires that the client be adequately identified by providing the required identifiers before any personal information is released.

- Authorization: the action or act of giving someone legal permission to access the file(s). The person authorized MUST still meet privacy by
The definitions as written above do not exist in WebCART. Simply ensure that participants understand the difference between both and that although Privacy may be met, the third party would need authorization in most circumstances.

current immigration status; educational institution; date permanent resident status was obtained; passport number; the office where the application was submitted; IPRMS receipt number;

number of persons included in the application; family members' names; employer's name; the amount paid for the application; etc.

Q5True or False – When reviewing a Bundle with various family member with the same email address, it is ok to respond as a Bundle even if they are not part of the same application.

# Q6List the steps to take when processing a Bundle in eGEM.

Answer

1. Evaluate each Webform's Applicant & Enquirer sections and perform the necessary privacy verification.
2. Verify the following:
a. Are all Webforms are for the same client?
b. If there are different clients, are they all part of the same application?
3. Once privacy/authorization are verified:
a. Upload documents or take necessary action (if applicable)
b. Draft a response containing information that the authorization provides for.

### Q9List the steps to unbundle in eGEM.

Answer

1. Navigate to the email you would like to separate and click the Remove From Bundle option.
2. A window will appear. Select one of the following options: Yes, return it: This option returns the Webform to inventory.
- No, keep it: this option return the Webform to your inbox for further processing and/or transfer.

## Q1 Enquiry:

REMINDER: submitted this on 26th May but no response received.

Application no : W123123123 Status: Refused due to error in IRCC system. Applicant Name : Whoopi Goldberg Program: Startup Visa Program.

1- I am applicant under startup visa program.

2 -My work permit application was refused (letter attached) on the ground that " I have not submitted permanent Resident application within time of validity of letter of support".

3- Please note that the basis of above refusal is wrong and due to an error in the IRCC system. I submit that i filed my PR application on the same data when i applied for the work permit with in valid period of letter of support.

# Q2Enquiry:

Amanda Nunes 222 Sandra street Thunder Bay, Ontario, L9T 6E9 anunes@shemail.com 647-555-2255 June 6, 2023

## To Whom It May Concern,

I am writing to support the temporary residence of my mother, Maria de Lourdes Nunes, in Ċanada. I am a Canadian citizen and the sole caregiver for my mother due to unfortunate circumstances. My father passed away, and my siblings are trapped on Mars due to the civil war.

Bringing my mother to Canada is in her best interest, as I can provide the necessary care, support, and love she requires. I am financially stable and capable of meeting her needs, as I hold a steady job with a reliable income.

My mother's health has deteriorated, necessitating constant medical attention and assistance with daily activities. With no close relatives in Brazil, where she currently resides, Canada offers access to the essential medical care she requires.

## I am fully aware of the complexities involved in the temporary residence process

and am prepared to fulfill all associated requirements and responsibilities. Attached are the necessary supporting documents, including proof of my Canadian citizenship and financial statements.

I kindly request your compassionate consideration of my mother's application. I assure you that I will shoulder all responsibilities, including financial obligations, and ensure her contribution as a productive member of Canadian society. Her

## presence will not only benefit my well-being but also enrich our multicultural

nation.

Answer

application.

# Hello,

I am getting worried about my access to RAMQ, since I need an application number and my interim work authorization form to get an extension of coverage while I wait for the actual visa to arrive (my current PGWP will expire September 8 2023).

I applied on paper due to a technical issue with my online portal--I received the Opt In emails and was told by phone by Melissa Lima (Agent #1234) to apply on paper to avoid having to pay fees I'm exempt from. I have attached information elaborating on my case and the technical issue that kept me from using the simple Opt In process despite my eligibility for the program.

Is there a way to expedite my application confirmation of receipt and issuance of application number? I just want to make sure I can maintain my Quebec health coverage while I wait for the actual visa to arrive.
