## Q7 Enquiry:

We are Counsel for Mr. Sean Paul, whose application for permanent residence (#E003456789) through the Express Entry was rejected on 2023/06/15. We hereby ask you to reconsider your decision and to re-open the application. The reason for the rejection appears to be that his Trinidad& Tobago police certificate was issued on 2021/01/20 and his last residence in this country was 2021/02/14. Please be advised that Mr. Paul left Trinidad on 2021/01/20 and has never gone back to this country. He went to Jamaica on that day and stayed in Jamaica until 2021/02/15 when he came to Canada. Hid trip to Jamaica was noted in his travel history. As he did not have a fixed address in Jamaica, he put his last address in Trinidad for the period of time he stayed in

# Jamaica. Between 2021/01/20 and 2021/02/14, he was physically living in

Jamaica, not in Trinidad. In our submission, we did explain that he has never gone back to Trinidad after his police certificate was issued. Please note this is the 2nd time Mr. Sean Paul was rejected a PR application and it has caused considerable stress to him. We apologize for the confusion about his address history and sincerely hope you will reopen his application. Copies of his Express Entry address/travel history and our explanation letter attached to the submitted Trinidad police certificate are all attached for your reference. Thank you very much for your reconsideration.

Answer

more details concerning the clients travel.

# Q2 True or False – You cannot attach documents if the enquirer is unauthorized.

Provide the path and section where this information can be found

Answer False. You can attach any document provided by unauthorized third parties (except for an LMIA/CAQ)

Procedures > Email Support Unit > Procedures-Documents (un)solicited received from clients > Before Proceeding

Q3What is the difference between a solicited and unsolicited document? Provide the path and section(s) where this information can be found

Answer A solicited document is a document that has been requested by an IRCC office for which a line was created in the Correspondence-Incoming view; If a line was not created in the Correspondence-Incoming view in ĠCMS to allow the client to upload this information, it will be considered as an unsolicited document. Procedures > Email Support Unit > Procedures-Documents (un)solicited received from clients > Document Solicited

An unsolicited document is a document or information that has not been requested by an IRCC office and that the client is sending as an additional document in support of their application; If a line was not created in the Correspondence-Incoming view in GCMS to allow the client to upload this information, then it will be considered as an unsolicited document. Procedures > Email Support Unit > Procedures-Documents (un)solicited received from clients > Document Unsolicited

Q4 Which type of document requires you add a document number when attaching to file? Provide the path where this information can be found?

Answer Passport Email Support Unit > Job aid-Creating an incoming correspondence and adding an attachment > Add an attachment

Procedures > Email Support Unit > Job aid-Creating an incoming correspondence and adding an attachment Q6 You verify a file and realize that another agent processed and responded to the Enquirer the same day. Which text would you send? Provide the path where this information can be found.

Answer If the enquiry is processed the same day that the action was taken:

- Initial duplicate request – reply to client using the text Duplicate request. Subsequent duplicate requests processed the same day – reject the email.
Procedures > Email Support Unit > Procedures-Processing Emails > Duplicate request or action already taken

Q7 You review a file and realize that another agent processed the enquirers request 45 days prior. Which text would you send?

Answer Action already taken-Less than 60 days

Procedures > Email Support Unit > Procedures-Processing Emails > Duplicate request or action already taken

Q8 The Enquirer is providing a document that has already been added to file 2 weeks prior. The file is being processed by New Delhi. Which text would you send?

Answer Action already taken – IR Procedures > Email Support Unit > Procedures-Processing Emails > Duplicate request or action already taken

Q9 When should you follow the Upload Mix-Up procedure? Provide the path where this information can be found.

Answer When you erroneously uploaded a client's document(s) to another client's file or notice a client's information uploaded in another client's file.

Q10 What steps should you take if you've upload both a correct and incorrect Webform to a file?

Answer If both a correct and an incorrect Web form were uploaded in an Incoming Correspondence: Create a new Incoming Correspondence with only the correct Web form; Process the Web form that was previously uploaded in error by: (if applicable), uploading it in the right file; and/or following the appropriate ESU procedure if the incorrect procedure was followed; Send the referral, Upload mix-up, including your team leader in Cc.

Procedures > Email Support Unit > Procedures-Processing Emails > Upload mix-up

Q11 Once you've ensured privacy is met, what is important to verify prior to attaching a Webform to a client's file?

Answer Verify if the document was previously added to file prior to attaching it.

Q12 True or False – You notice that a client's LMIA was added to their PR application instead of their WP application. This is an Upload Mix-Up.

Answer

## False. Upload Mix-Up should only be followed if there is a chance of a privacy

breach. In this scenario, the expectation is that you change the IC status to cancelled and upload the LMIA to the correct WP application.

the related note?

If you send a referral and then enter the related note at a later date, please enter the date the referral was sent in your note.

The game plan is provided to the ESU unit Monday to Friday prior to 8h30. The ESU unit will receive the email indicating the contentious Line of Business (LOB) they must process and in which system, Alpha or eGem.

# Reconsideration

Reconsideration requests are processed daily by team leaders only. We receive these emails in outlook only, via referrals from across the CSC. It is very important that these referrals be treated within 24hrs of receipt as this represents a potential volatile situation for our applicants. Please see below the steps to follow when processing these situations.

## ALPHA

1. open folder “CE CSC Question Intake / Reception Question CAC CE (IRCC)
2. Expand the inventory folder
## CE CSC Question Intake / Reception Qu. _Emails Message Have Been changed

Inbox [16] Inventory Sent Items

### Deleted Items [279] Drafts

### PRC Urgent [71] Public Policy for Workers

Privacy pass pilot PRIVACY TEST (ignore this folder) Reconsideration Request [1] Resolved Webform [48] RPA- rejected e-mails

3. Open GCMS and reconfirm privacy, application etc
4. Review referral if all information needed is indicated to locate the webform in question
5. Verify archived emails in alpha and egem to verify if we did receive webform as per what client indicates in email (view retrieving returned IC for procedure to locate archived emails)
6. Follow correct procedure in webcart for referral to be actioned correctly [http://cicwebcart/agentCorners/callcentre/index-eng.aspx](http://cicwebcart/agentCorners/callcentre/index-eng.aspx)

# Returning Referrals

Description Before Proceeding

- Procedure(s) Returning a referral
- Referrals •Script(s)
- Further Info
DESCRIPTION

While reviewing a file, you notice a note from an agent who sent:

- A referral containing errors or that is incomplete and may lead to a misunderstanding by the concerned office;
- A referral that should not have been sent; or
- The wrong referral.
BEFORE PROCEEDING

- Access the CE CSC Operations / Opérations CSC EC (IRCC) mailbox and retrieve the referral;
- Verify if it was reviewed already: the information about a previous review will appear in the follow-up flag;
- Verify if it was approved by a Program Specialists Unit (PSU) agent: this information will usually appear next to, or below, the signature of the person who sent the referral.
PROCEDURE(S)

7. Should the referral be incomplete of needed information to process correctly, follow the correct scenario in the webcart procedure.
8. To locate the correct unit and or team leader of the agent that sent the original referral, open the G drive, open “outils partager" then select “repartition d’equipes".

##### IMM 5475/5476 - Stale dated

The signature date on the form is more than 90 calendar days:

## If the form is

dated...

#### 90 calendar days or

less BEFORE the application reception date

#### Anytime AFTER the

application reception date

# Then...

### You can accept it, even if you don't have proof that it was

submitted initially with the application.

- The form must be signed 90 calendar days ago or less;
exception: you can accept a form that was signed more than 90 calendar days ago if you have proof that the form was initially sent within 90 calendar days from the signature date (e.g., the form was uploaded by the office in the IMM/CIT screen> Correspondence-Incoming or e- Docs view, a Party ID was created in the IMM/CIT screen > Client & Parties view, a note was added by the office, etc.); this includes when the requester resubmits an authorization form with a slight modification.

After consulting the above table, you determine the form is stale dated:

Reply to requester using the text IMM 5475 or 5476 - Stale dated-Form dated beyond 90 days; and Add a note:

CSC received (IMM 5475 OR IMM 5476) by (email OR fax). Request refused as form was signed more than(90 calendar days ago OR more-than 90 calendar days before the application reception date). Emailed (client OR rep) at (enter email address) to advise to send new form with updated signature. INIT-ID

IMM 5475/5476 - Outdated or previous version of form

##### IMM 5475 form:

001563

Immigration, Refugees 串 and Citizenship Canadaet Citoyenneté Canada Information disclosed under the Access to Information Act Lnomdn

Any new IMM 5475 form received must be the current version from May 2021 in English and French. Previous versions are no longer accepted.

##### IMM 5476 form:

The IMM 5476 form was updated in November 2021 in English and French.

Starting April 1, 2022, any IMM 5476 received for a newly submitted application must be the current version from November 2021. Any previous versions of the form will no longer be accepted as of that date for any newly submitted application.

Although using the current version of the IMM 5476 form is encouraged, some previous versions may be accepted for applications submitted before Mareh 31, 2022:

The previous version from Oetober 2021 is acceptable until Mareh 31, 2022; The prior version from July 2021 is acceptable until December 31, 2021; e any other previous version is outdated and must not be accepted.

Note:

- If a representative Party ID line is cancelled and indicates No longer Representative in the Disassoc Reason field in GCMS due to the representative linking the application to their online account, the procedures in this section are still applicable for adding a representative or designated individual as long as it is for the same person indicated in the Party ID line and all the information matches.
For applications submitted on or after April 1, 2022 (for an IMM 5476) OR May 1, 2021 (for an IMM 5475):

## 2. New page

# Robotic Process Automation (RPA) - Uncoupling strategy

### Uncoupling strategy

As of November 14, 2023, when a client chooses to upload documents for these types of applications/enquiries, the free text box will no longer appear:

Replacement documents, Amendments to documents and Verification of Status documents Citizenship Permanent Resident Card Sponsorship Temporary Residence (applied online) Temporary Residence (applied by mail) In-Canada Permanent Residence Permanent Residence (applied online) Permanent Residence (applied by mail)

If they need to provide additional information about the documents being submitted, they will be invited to upload a letter of explanation with the documents.

If the client wishes to ask general or case-specific questions after uploading the documents, they can submit a new Web form. To do so, they will need to select No when answering the question Do you want to add a document to an existing file/application?.

001821

Immigration, Refugees and Cizenship Canedaot Citoyenneté Canada Information disclosed under the Access to Information Act L'information divulguée en vertu de la oi sur l’accès à Finformation

## All other types of applications/enquiries remain unchanged. Client can still upload documents and ask

general or case-specific questions in the same Web form.

How does RPA work?

RPA analyses the following information in the Web form:

- If there is an attachment; and
- If there is a valid application number.
If the criteria is met:

- RPA runs through an automated process of searching for the application in GCMS; If the application is found: it will upload the attachment(s) to the application number provided in the Web form; 0for more information on visualizing a correspondence line created by RPA, consult this
job aid and this Further Info section; One of the following email tags will be added to the Web form which will be processed in

# Alpha:

RPA-UPLOADED-SOLICITED: if the office created a line for the client to provide the requested documents in the Correspondence-Incoming view; RPA-UPLOADED-UNSOLICITED: if client is providing documents that were not requested by the office and there is no line available to upload them in the Correspondence-Incoming view;

- If there is a valid email address in the Web form, RPA will automatically send the auto-reply to that email address; the date the auto-reply was sent is located in the Categories field, under the Item
Properties section in Alpha;

- If the email address provided is invalid, an error message will appear; in this case, the documents will be attached on file and clients will not get a response.

# (OK until)

DESCRIPTION

# (OK until)

*Canadian passports and travel documents, citizenship certificates, permanent resident cards and Refugee Protection Claimant Documents (RPCD).

Temporary residents (TR) can be issued a TR document with an X gender identifier if the passport issued by their country has an X-Another gender (or an equivalent, non-binary option) in the Sex or Gender field.

The gender identifier cannot be changed on an Electronic Travel Authorization (eTA) document, as it is linked to the person's foreign passport or travel document. Once they have updated the sex or gender on that document, they ean will be able to apply for a new eTA.

### Online tools and services

For online tools and services, the mandate of the CSC's ESU agents is to respond by email to clients who require support with the use of an online tool or service or need assistance because of a technical problem, whether their application is/will be processed inside or outside of Canada:

- See the appropriate section in this procedure; if you cannot find the answer in the appropriate section, follow thís procedure.

### Correspondence Received

# Error

Description Before Proceeding Procedure(s) CPC-M-Recommendation Letter CPC-S-Acknowledgement Letter CPC-E/Local Office-Approval in Principle Letter Medical Instruction Letter, Biometric Instruction Letter (BIL) or Passport Reguest

## Letter

Referrals Incoming correspondence eGEM Further Info

### DESCRIPTION

Client says the CPC or local office made an error in their information on a correspondence they received:

Recommendation letter Acknowledgement letter Approval in principle letter Medical instruction letter Biometric instruction letter (BIL) Passport request letter

#### BEFORE PROCEEDING

- For an error on an IRcc document: follow the corresponding procedure in the appropriate CHD (CTZ, IMM or PRC). •If the client received another client's correspondence:
follow this procedure.

### Determine the source of the error;

Verify which office sent the correspondence and which office currently holds the file.

#### IMPORTANT (for IMM applications):

001949

Immigration, Refugees 中 and Cizenship Canedaet Citoyenneté Canada Information disclosed under the Access to Information Act L'information divulguée en vertu de la loi sur laccès à Finformnation

Ask client to verify if their surname and given name are entered in separate fields on their passport. As per OB 051, if the client's surname and given name are entered in the same field:

Both names will appear in the Surname field; and This is not considered an error.

## PROCEDURE(S)

Visa office indicated on the BELA letter is not where the file is/was CPC-M-Recommendation transferred to (i.e., different from what GCMS indicates): Letter Verify if a second BELA letter was already sent to the client;

- If no other BELA letter was sent:
add an incoming correspondence (Item: Urgent Request) for CPC-M by following these instructions; and advise client that CPC-M will send a second letter indicating the correct visa office;

- If the error also appears in the Clients screen in GCMS: follow this procedure as well.

# Change of sex or gender identifier

Description

## Before proceeding

Procedure(s) General enquiries-X gender identifier Changing sex or gender identifier Referrals Incoming correspondence eGEM Further info

DESCRIPTION

A person inquires about adding a different sex or gender identifier (i.e., F, M or X) in the Sex or Gender field of IRCC-issued documents* and wants to:

Obtain information of general nature regarding the X gender identifier; or Know how to proceed to add a different sex or gender identifier.

*Canadian passports and travel documents, citizenship certificates, permanent resident cards and Refugee Protection Claimant Documents (RPCD).

Temporary residents (TR) can be issued a TR document with an X gender identifier if the passport issued by their country has an X-Another gender (or an equivalent, non-binary option) in the Sex or Gender field.

The gender identifier cannot be changed on an Electronic Travel Authorization (eTA) document, as it is linked to the person's foreign passport or travel document. Once they have updated the sex or gender on that document, they can apply for a new eTA.

# SCRIPT(S):

eGEM scripts

IRCC no longer sending application forms and kits

"Sending paper forms by mail is not possible anymore at IRCC. If you would like, I can send you a link to the application kit on our website. All you will have to do is to download the guide and the forms or, for certain types of applications, you may also be able to apply online."

Reasons why IRCC decided to no longer send application forms and kits by mail

"Since December 1, 2011, IRCC is no longer printing and mailing out application forms and kits in an effort to:

Ensure applicants have the most recent forms available; Save costs to better serve clients; and Reduce our impact on the environment.

However, you have other great options such as downloading application kits directly from the IRCC

## website or applying online (for certain types of applications). Furthermore, our website will also give

you access to our Help Centre to get answers to your questions."

Client has no access to the internet

"I understand that access to a computer may not be available to everyone. Do you have friends or family who can help you download and print the forms? If you do not have access to the internet, you can contact your local community centre or library to see if you they are able to print the forms for you at these locations. As another option, you may also be able to pay for the service at a commercial copy centre."

# Adding postal delivery times before sending a referral or adding an incoming

correspondence:

For a situation which involves any kind of correspondence being sent by mail: add 7 business days (postal delivery times) to the processing times indicated in the procedure or on the IRCC website.

Unless specified in the procedure, the required action must be directed to the destination office under the History view > Paper File Transfer by clicking on the Transfer ID hyperlink.

### Client wants to know:

How to send the supporting documents they forgot to include with their application; or If a note can be added on file because they already sent the supporting documents to the office.

### BEFORE PROCEEDING

## Verify:

What type of application was submitted; Which office will process/is processing the application; What document was not included; and If client already sent it or not.

Important:

### If the missing document:

Is a photocopy of a current/expired immigration document and it already is in the system, no action is required; Has already been requested through the online account, follow the e-application procedure; Is a mandatory requirement as per the document checklist (i.e., R10), follow the appropriate procedure below; otherwise, if the missing document is not mandatory, the procedure below is not applicable as its purpose is to avoid having an application returned to a client (without

## offering any guarantees).

## PROCEDURE(S)

WP Follow this procedure. application-LMIA/CAQ not included

Any other Application does not exist in the system:

2. Referrals, Incoming Correspondences, Notes and Comments-Guidelines CHD > Useful Info > Referrals, Incoming Correspondences, Notes and Comments – Guidelines
## Referrals, Incoming Correspondences, Notes and Comments-Guidelines

When writing notes, emails or comments, or adding an incoming correspondence, please adhere to the following guidelines:

Language Writing text Adding notes on file Adding an incoming correspondence If an action is required Third party and notes/comments on file

# Language

In order to avoid possible delays because of translation, referrals and notes should be in the language most used by the concerned office:

- French or English for the Quebec region; and
002084

Immigration, Refugees and Cizenship Canedaet Citoyenneté Canada Information disclosed under the Access to Information Act L'information divulguée en vertu de la oi sur l’accès à Finformation

- English for the other regions.
## For Incoming correspondences-Comment box:

- Use the language specified in the CHD instructions to ensure offices are able to process the CSC's requests in GCMS. The option to add the Incoming correspondences in French is available for designated bilingual offices.
# Writing text

- When asked to provide a brief description or the pertinent points of a situation, be brief and to the point;
do include, however, sufficient information to ensure understanding of the situation or the response given to a client.

- Do not use all capitals, large, bold, underlined or red text, as these formats are generally used to express anger or insistence.
- Do not include opinions or comments, only the facts.
# Adding notes on file

- The note should be entered for each applicant's file, if applicable.
- Multiple notes can be combined if the CHD indicates they are to be entered in the same screen.
## Adding an incoming correspondence

Consult the guidelines provided in this Job Aid.

# If an action is required

- If an action is taken, after having advised client of the action taken, please use the following script: I will send a message to the responsible office for the file to be reviewed. Please rest assured that the appropriate action will be taken on your file.
Note: As per this procedure, a single follow-up message can only be sent after 60 days,
 unless indicated otherwise. Please avoid inviting client to call back in 60 days, unless they ask when they can call back.
- If an action is required on file, it must be taken right after the call.
- Subject title in the referral must not be modified (unless otherwise specified in the CHD).
002085

Immigration, Réfugiés Immigration, Refugees and Citizenshi Canada et Ciloyeneé Canada Information disclosed under the Access to Information Act L'information divulguée en vertu de la loi sur laccès à Finformation

- If a procedure instructs you to add an incoming correspondence but there is no application in the system, send the appropriate referral instead.
- Send one referral if your action concerns more than one member of the same family.
## Third party and notes/comments on file

When a third party (e.g., paid/unpaid representative, dependent listed on the application, power of attorney, etc.) contacts the Client Support Centre and:

- A referral is sent: indicate the name of that person in the Caller's name field; and
- adjust the note to indicate that a third party called;
- for example:
# Third party contacted CSC. Referral, No application in system, for (enter type

of application) sent to XX to verify since processing times have passed. INIT-ID

OR

- An incoming correspondence is added: a note to this effect must be added in the incoming correspondence comment(s);
for example:

Caller is different than client: *Provide name*

### Client refuses to sign representative cancellation form

In cases where the client refuses to sign a cancellation form, representatives may ask to be removed from a file if they provide:

A duly filled IMM 5476 form, with the exception that client signature is not necessary; and An explanation letter that contains information about: why the representative wants to be removed from the file; and why the client will not/cannot sign the form.

### Paid representative holds a restrictive permit

●Representative/the governing body's website indicates which areas of law they are authorized to practice in: there are no restrictions related to immigration law: add the IMM 5476 as per this procedure; and add a note in the Parties screen > Notes view:

Received IMM 5476 for paid representative (enter name of rep). Rep holds a restrictive permit that authorizes them to practice immigration law. Authorization added in (enter type of application). INIT-ID.

- there are restrictions related to immigration law:
reply to requester using the text Authorization-Restrictive permit-Refused; and add a note in the Parties screen > Notes view:

Received IMM 5476 for paid representative (enter name of rep). Rep holds a restrictive permit that does not authorize them to practice immigration law. Authorization refused. INIT-ID

Representative/the governing body's website does not indicate which areas of law they are authorized to practice in: reply to requester using the text Authorization-Restrictive permit-Clarifications required; and add a note in the Parties screen > Notes view:

Received IMM 5476 for paid representative (enter name of rep). Rep holds a restrictive permit. Email sent to (enter email address) to clarify restrictions. INIT-ID

## Incomplete IMM 5475/5476

### If an email address was provided:

- If the entire form is unreadable:
- reply to requester using the text IMM 5475 or 5476 - Refused-Unreadable.
- If certain sections of the form are incomplete and/or unreadable:
- reply to requester using the text IMM 5475 or 5476 - Incomplete;
and

- Add a note in the system: Received incomplete (IMM 5475 or IMM 5476) (enter reason) by (email OR fax). Emailed (client OR rep) at (enter email address). INIT-ID
If NO email address was provided on the fax:

- Contact the requester via telephone to obtain the missing information: Hello, my name is (your name). I am calling from Immigration, Refugees and Citizenship
002105

Immigration, Refugees and Cizenship Canedaet Citoyenneté Canada Information disclosed under the Access to Information Act L'information divulguée en vertu de la oi sur l’accès à Finformation

Canada in regards to an incomplete Use of representative form received for (name of rep) for (name of applicant). In order to process your request, you must resend the IRCC Web form with the corrected information XX. I can send you the information by email. Can I please have your email address?

- Send requester the appropriate text: if the entire form is unreadable:
send requester the text IMM 5475 or 5476 - Refused-Unreadable.

- if certain sections of the form are incomplete and/or unreadable:

## CoR Letter

This is in reference to your online application for a temporary resident visa. Please be advised that at this time your application is currently in progress. Before we can proceed with your request we kindly ask you to reply via your MyCIC account and advise our office what your current country of residence is and where you would like to submit your passport once it is requested. The above information must be received in our office within 10 days from the date of this letter. You

#### must upload the requested documents/information by using your IRCC secure account within the time

frame indicated above.

Thank you for the interest you have shown in Canada.

#### CoR Letter (FRENCH)

La présente concerne votre demande en ligne de visa de résident temporaire. Veuillez noter qu’à l’heure actuelle, votre candidature est en cours de traitement. Avant de pourvoir traiter votre demande, nous vous demandons de bien vouloir répondre via votre compte MyCIC et d’informer notre bureau de votre pays de résidence actuel et de l’endroit où vous souhaitez soumettre votre passeport une fois qu’il est demandé. Les informations ci-dessus doivent être reçues à notre bureau dans les 10 jours suivant la date de cette lettre. Vous devez télécharger les documents/informations demandes en utilisant votre compte sécurisé IRCC dans les délais indiqués ci-dessus.

#### Nous vous remercions de l'intérêt que vous avez manifesté pour le Canada.

#### time when the application will be finalized.

# Application Validity:

Thank you for contacting Immigration, Refugees and Citizenship Canada (IRCC).

Although Temporary Resident Visas can be issued up to the end of the validity of the passport or for 10 years, whichever is shorter, the actual validity of the visa is determined by the officer based on a full assessment of all the information available. The validity of the visa issued may therefore be shorter than the maximum. In such cases, a new visa would not be issued for a longer validity.

# Application Validity (FRENCH)

## Merci d'avoir contacté Immigration, Réfugiés et Citoyenneté Canada (IRCC).

Bien que les visas de résident temporaire peuvent être délivrés jusqu'à la fin de la validité du passeport ou pour 10 ans, selon la période la plus courte, la validité réelle du visa est déterminée par l'agent sur la base d'une évaluation complète de toutes les informations disponibles. La validité du visa délivré peut donc être plus courte que le maximum. Dans de tels cas, un nouveau visa ne serait pas délivré pour une validité plus longue.

Nous vous remercions de l'intérêt que vous avez manifesté pour le Canada.

### Reconsideration/Refusal:

Please note that an assessment of each application is made on its merits. A written decision was provided

### containing the factors considered for refusing the application for temporary residence, thereby concluding

the application. The decision is final and there are no provisions for appeal to this office. Should you have different or additional information, you may submit a new application for a temporary

### resident visa with appropriate fees.

For more information, please visit our website at: [http://www.cic.gc.ca/english/information/applications/visa.asp](http://www.cic.gc.ca/english/information/applications/visa.asp)

## Thank you for your understanding,

# Reconsideration/Refusal (FRENCH):

Veuillez noter que l'évaluation de chaque demande est faite en fonction de son bien-fondé. Une décision écrite a été fournie contenant les facteurs pris en compte pour refuser la demande de résidence temporaire, concluant ainsi la demande. La décision est définitive et il n'y a aucune possibilité d'appel auprès de ce bureau. Si vous disposez d'informations différentes ou supplémentaires, vous pouvez soumettre une nouvelle

### demande de visa de résident temporaire accompagnée des droits appropriés.

## Pour plus d'informations, veuillez consulter notre site web à l'adresse suivante : [https://www.cic.gc.ca/francais/information/demandes/visa.asp](https://www.cic.gc.ca/francais/information/demandes/visa.asp)

# Validity Determined by Several Factors

## Thank you for your enquiry.

The validity date of a visitor visa is determined by several factors and not primarily on the duration of valid biometrics. I would ask that you follow the instructions provided in the passport request letter and kindly provide your passports to us within the required timeframe. Once the visa that has currently been issued to you expires, should you wish to apply for a subsequent visa, you will be required to submit a new application, along with the required processing fees.

For more information, please visit our website at: [http://www.cic.gc.ca/english/information/applications/visa.asp](http://www.cic.gc.ca/english/information/applications/visa.asp)

## Thank you for your understanding.

## Applicant Requesting Additional Information on Refusal

Please be advised that a visa decision cannot normally be changed once it is taken. This office is unable to provide information or details regarding the reasons for a refusal other than the information contained in the refusal letter.

If there is new information not previously considered or if you believe circumstance have changed since the decision was made, you can file a new application and pay the processing fee. A different officer will review this new application. However, if there is no change in the applicant's situation, then it is unlikely that the application will result in the issuance of a visa.

Please know that although an inviter may provide documentation in support of an application of a friend, relative, or business contact, the decision of the visa officer is ultimately based on the applicant's circumstances, and not on any personal or financial guarantees that and inviter may be prepared to offer on that persons behalf.

An applicant may also seek leave through the Federal Court of Canada to request a judicial review of any decision made by a visa officer, if he or she believes that the process was not legally or procedurally fair. A lawyer in Canada would have to act on behalf of the applicant.

Thank you for your understanding.

## Urgent processing Request (Documents received)

This is in reference to your online application for a temporary resident visa, and further to your enquiry.

Please be advised that at this time your application is currently in progress and that your request for urgent processing and supporting documents have been received. Before we can proceed with your request we kindly ask you to reply via your MyCIC account and advise our office which option better suits your needs:

- You may choose to remain in Canada while you submit your passport at our Case Processing Centre Ottawa/Gatineau.
Or You may choose to travel immediately and we could transfer your application to the Canadian Visa Office abroad of your choice or responsible for the country where you would be residing. For that we would require your written consent that you want us to transfer your application to the Visa Office abroad, please also state the name of the office or country where you would be residing. Please also advise our office if you have already traveled outside of Canada.

If you choose to travel while your application is still in progress please note that we cannot guarantee the processing times at the Visa Offices abroad, they will be able to guide your regarding the next steps.

Please submit your response at your earliest convenience in order for us to accommodate your request in a timely manner.

You may also contact the Client Service Centre, toll-free from within Canada, at 1-888-242-2100.

# Apply for a new TRV

Thank you for contacting Immigration, Refugees and Citizenship Canada (IRCC). Please note, since you applied for a new work permit after your Temporary Resident Visa (TRV), your TRV matches that of the old work permit, which was valid until 2021-9-07. Therefore, since you have applied for a new work permit extension, you must re-apply for a new TRV.

0Forelgn students and workers who got a temporary resident (visitor) visa in Canada If there is a V-1 code on your visitor visa This is not an error. Oue to COviD-19, we have temporary measures in place to process visitor visa applications submitted from inside Canada.

- Foreign students and workers who applied for a visitor visa in Canada since juty 2021 may get a V-1 visitor visa
- You can use your V-1 visa to leave and re -enter Canada-the same way you would use your S-1 or W-1 visa.
- You don't need to request a different type of visa or tollow up with the processing office or client support centre. A new visa will not be issued in this case.
Validity period is different on the V-1 visa

- The V-1 visa is valid until the expiration date of your passport. This is part of the temporary measures in place that wil be in effect until further notice If your y-1 visa expires. you can apply for a new visitor visa.
Issuance of your temporary resident visa to passport validity, coded as a V-1

Your application for a temporary resident visa (TRV) has been approved and a counterfoil with the code V-1 has been affixed to your passport, which will expire the day before your current passport.

Previously, foreign nationals in Canada who had a valid study or work permit were often issued a TRV document with the coding that corresponded to their permit (i.e. S-1 or W-1). In response to COVID-19 though, IRCC is issuing these TRVs with the code V-1 to facilitate processing. Please do not re-apply to change the code on your TRV, a new document will not be issued to you.

As long as you continue to meet all the requirements, you can use this TRV to leave Canada and re-enter at a later date.

Please note, as part of the regular processing, a border services officer will make the final determination of your entry to Canada at the port of entry.

### For more information, refer to our Help Centre:

I have a visitor visa, can I leave Canada and come back? [https://ircc.canada.ca/english/helpcentre/answer.asp?qnum=208&top=22](https://ircc.canada.ca/english/helpcentre/answer.asp?qnum=208&top=22)

Thank you for the interest you have shown in Canada.

### Issuance of your temporary resident visa to passport validity, coded as a V-1

## (FRENCH)

Votre demande de visa de résident temporaire (VRT) a été approuvée. Une vignette portant le code V-1 a été apposée sur votre passeport et expirera le jour précédant ce dernier.

Auparavant, les étrangers au Canada qui avaient un permis d’études ou de travail valide se voyaient

### souvent délivrer un VRT portant le code correspondant à leur permis (c’est-à-dire S-1 ou W-1).

Cependant, en raison de là COVID-19, IRCC délivre ces VRT avec le code V-1 pour faciliter le traitement. Veuillez ne pas faire une nouvelle demande pour changer le code sur votre VRT; un nouveau document ne vous sera pas délivré.

Tant que vous cont nuez à satisfaire à toutes les exigences, vous pouvez utiliser ce VRT pour quitter le Canada et y revenir à une date ultérieure.

Veuillez noter que, dans le cadre du traitement normal, un agent des services frontaliers au point d’entrée prendra la décision finale quant à votre entrée au Canada.

Pour de plus amples renseignements, consultez notre Centre d’aide : J'ai un visa de visiteur. Puis-je quitter le Canada et revenir? [https://ircc.canada.ca/francais/centre-aide/reponse.asp?qnum=208&top=22](https://ircc.canada.ca/francais/centre-aide/reponse.asp?qnum=208&top=22)

Nous vous remercions de l’intérêt que vous avez manifesté envers le Canada.

(Client is asking about counterfoil category and validity) Please be advised that our office has received your inquiry regarding the visa that was granted in your passport. Please be advised that your visa was issued with a V-1 counterfoil category instead of a counterfoil category matching the permit that you hold to the validity of your passport. This was intentional and that having a V-1 counterfoil category will allow you to use your visa with the current permit you hold.

Thank you for the interest you have shown in Canada.

# Error on Visa expiration

Upon review of the application, it has been established that validity date of your current temporary resident visa (TRV) is not erroneous. Kindly note that validity dates are established on several factors and requirements applicable at that the time of issuance. You will be able to apply for a new TRV once the

### current valid TRV will expire.

Please note that the validity date on the TRV is decided at the processing officer's discretion based on case by case review. You will need to re-apply for a new application to be given a TRV of a longer validity.

Thank you for the interest you have shown in Canada.

## VH-1 Cruise ship Cancellation

As you wish to travel by cruise, your purpose of travel is not eligible for a transit visa. As a result, your application for a transit visa has been withdrawn. You may wish to consider applying for a temporary resident visa (tourist visa) instead, and paying the respective processing fees. For more information, please visit www.cic.gc.ca.
